# Unity requirements and evidence

This index covers the source brief in `Assets/PROMPT.md`. Each row starts as **Pending**. Use **Partial** for implementation with incomplete coverage, and **Verified** only when the listed evidence supports the full row. The Section 24 labels use the original numeric items prefixed with 24. Older world and catalog totals are retained as source requirements, with V2 superseding those numeric targets.

## Victory predicates

| ID | Medal | Gate | Source | Status | Unity implementation / evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| D1 | Desert World | Every one of 2,359,296 eligible cells passes stable-desert predicate during audit | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4128) | Pending | — |
| D2 | No Trees | 0 living tree entities / viable standing saplings worldwide; no regrowth-enabled viable seed bank | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4129) | Pending | — |
| D3 | Dry Hydrosphere | Every freshwater lake/river/wetland surface store below dry epsilon; every required aquifer/watershed under depletion threshold; water available to living ecosystems effectively zero | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4130) | Pending | — |
| D4 | Carbon Overload | Fictional atmospheric CO₂ **burden** meets global campaign cap and has been at/above it over stability window | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4131) | Pending | — |
| D5 | Pollution Saturation | Every required region meets multi-pollutant contamination threshold; global accumulated pollutant index meets cap, with no untouched clean refuge | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4132) | Pending | — |
| D6 | Soil Sterility | No eligible cell retains viable fertile topsoil or sufficient soil health/organic carbon for spontaneous recovery | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4133) | Pending | — |
| D7 | Heat Collapse | Regional temperature/anomaly thresholds passed in every modeled province and global thermal stress target met over stability window | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4134) | Pending | — |
| D8 | Wildlife Exit | Every province has lost persistent wildlife habitat suitability and remaining wild animal populations are at the game-defined extinction threshold | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L4135) | Pending | — |

| 24.11 | Currency is deposited only after the sell/burn job succeeds. | [§](Assets/PROMPT.md#L1881) | Verified | [`Assets/Clearcut`](Assets/Clearcut); [Unity smoke report](Builds/Evidence/opening-smoke.txt) |
